<meta name="body" content="&lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;Last updated: June 2026&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;row&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;col-xs-12 col-sm-7&quot;&gt;&lt;article class=&quot;media media--type-remote-video media--view-mode-ratio-16-9-blazy&quot;&gt;

&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;col-xs-12 col-sm-5&quot;&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;promo-card&quot;&gt;&lt;article class=&quot;media media--type-image media--view-mode-ratio-3&quot;&gt;

    &lt;div class=&quot;field__label visually-hidden&quot;&gt;Image&lt;/div&gt;
              &lt;div class=&quot;field__item&quot;&gt;  &lt;img loading=&quot;lazy&quot; src=&quot;/sites/default/files/styles/ratio_3_1/public/2025-04/Unitary_Patent_Social_Media_Campaign_Ask.UP_Still_Cropped.jpg?h=b09aae90&amp;itok=148G9VxV&quot; width=&quot;1920&quot; height=&quot;640&quot; alt=&quot;Decorative image&quot; class=&quot;image-style-ratio-3-1&quot;&gt;


&lt;/div&gt;
          &lt;/div&gt;

  &lt;/article&gt;
&lt;div class=&quot;promo-card-header&quot;&gt;&lt;h3&gt;Ask.UP&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;promo-card-content&quot;&gt;&lt;p&gt;Mini web-series where we clarify the most common questions about the Unitary Patent&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;promo-card-footer&quot;&gt;&lt;a class=&quot;secondary-button button-icon icon-link_external&quot; href=&quot;https://www.youtube.com/playlist?list=PLgDlswd0__eJRyOeAwG20xJ3i3DtyUzx9&quot; role=&quot;button&quot;&gt;Watch now&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;European patents with unitary effect registered by the EPO on or after 1 September 2024 will take effect in the 18 EU Member States participating in enhanced co‑operation and having ratified the Agreement on a Unified Patent Court (UPC Agreement) by that date. It is expected that more EU Member States will ratify the UPC Agreement in the coming years, so that eventually Unitary Patents will make it possible to get patent protection in up to &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.epo.org/en/applying/european/unitary/unitary-patent/legal-framework&quot;&gt;25 EU Member States&lt;/a&gt; by submitting a single request to the EPO.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;The new Unitary Patent is based on the European patent granted by the EPO under the rules of the &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.epo.org/law-practice/legal-texts/epc.html&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;European Patent Convention (EPC)&lt;/a&gt;, so nothing changes in the pre-grant phase and the same high standards of quality search and examination apply. After a European patent is granted, the patent proprietor can request unitary effect, thereby getting a European patent with unitary effect (Unitary Patent) that provides uniform patent protection in 18 EU Member States.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;inline-buttons&quot;&gt;&lt;a class=&quot;primary-button button-icon mdi mdi-arrow-right&quot; href=&quot;https://www.epo.org/en/service-support/faq/law-practice/unitary-patent&quot; role=&quot;button&quot; tabindex=&quot;-1&quot;&gt;Frequently asked questions about the Unitary Patent&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;h2&gt;Territorial scope of European patents and those with unitary effect&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;dl class=&quot;ckeditor-accordion&quot;&gt;&lt;dt&gt;Member states of the EPO (40)&lt;/dt&gt;&lt;dd&gt;&lt;article class=&quot;media media--type-image media--view-mode-ratio-16-9-blazy&quot;&gt;
  
      
            &lt;div class=&quot;blazy blazy--photoswipe blazy--lightbox blazy--photoswipe-gallery blazy--field blazy--field-media-image blazy--field-media-image--ratio-16-9-blazy field field--name-field-media-image field--type-image field--label-hidden field__item&quot; data-photoswipe-gallery data-blazy&gt;




          &lt;a href=&quot;/sites/default/files/2026-06/Map%20WEB%20No%20CC%20-%20Official%20EPO%20Member%20States.jpg&quot; class=&quot;blazy__photoswipe litebox litebox--image&quot; data-photoswipe-trigger data-b-media=&quot;{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;photoswipe&quot;,&quot;count&quot;:1,&quot;boxType&quot;:&quot;image&quot;,&quot;type&quot;:&quot;image&quot;,&quot;token&quot;:&quot;b-e74b1e2e4fe&quot;,&quot;paddingHack&quot;:true,&quot;width&quot;:1920,&quot;height&quot;:1080}&quot;&gt;    &lt;div data-b-token=&quot;b-e74b1e2e4fe&quot; class=&quot;media media--blazy media--switch media--switch--photoswipe media--image is-b-loading&quot;&gt;&lt;img alt=&quot;Map showing the potential geographic coverage of European patents&quot; decoding=&quot;async&quot; class=&quot;media__element b-lazy&quot; loading=&quot;lazy&quot; data-src=&quot;/sites/default/files/styles/ratio_16_9/public/2026-06/Map%20WEB%20No%20CC%20-%20Official%20EPO%20Member%20States.jpg?h=920929c4&amp;itok=utbdQaJ3&quot; src=&quot;data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%201%201'%2F%3E&quot; width=&quot;1920&quot; height=&quot;1080&quot;&gt;
        &lt;span class=&quot;media__icon media__icon--litebox&quot;&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/a&gt;
        
        
  &lt;/div&gt;
      
  &lt;/article&gt;
&lt;p&gt;&lt;br&gt;The EPO currently has &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.epo.org/en/about-us/foundation/member-states&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;40 member states&lt;/a&gt;, in addition to &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.epo.org/en/about-us/foundation/extension-states&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;one extension state&lt;/a&gt; and &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.epo.org/en/about-us/foundation/validation-states&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;five validation states&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/dd&gt;&lt;dt&gt;States in enhanced co-operation (25)&lt;/dt&gt;&lt;dd&gt;&lt;article class=&quot;media media--type-image media--view-mode-original-dimensions-no-blazy&quot;&gt;
  
      
            &lt;div class=&quot;field field--name-field-media-image field--type-image field--label-hidden field__item&quot;&gt;  &lt;img loading=&quot;lazy&quot; src=&quot;/sites/default/files/styles/original_dimensions/public/2025-08/Official%20EPO%20Map_1920x1080px_EU25_0.jpg?itok=ciW2Czws&quot; width=&quot;4000&quot; height=&quot;2250&quot; alt=&quot;States in enhanced cooperation (25)&quot; class=&quot;image-style-original-dimensions&quot;&gt;


&lt;/div&gt;
      
  &lt;/article&gt;
&lt;p&gt;&lt;br&gt;The 25 states participating in &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.epo.org/en/applying/european/unitary/unitary-patent/legal-framework&quot;&gt;enhanced cooperation&lt;/a&gt; to bring about the Unitary Patent system are: Austria, Belgium, Bulgaria, Cyprus, Czech Republic, Denmark, Estonia, Finland, France, Germany, Greece, Hungary, Ireland, Italy, Latvia, Lithuania, Luxembourg, Malta, the Netherlands, Poland, Portugal, Romania, Slovakia, Slovenia, Sweden.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/dd&gt;&lt;dt&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Current states in the UP and UPC system (18)&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/dt&gt;&lt;dd&gt;&lt;article class=&quot;media media--type-image media--view-mode-original-dimensions-no-blazy&quot;&gt;
  
      
            &lt;div class=&quot;field field--name-field-media-image field--type-image field--label-hidden field__item&quot;&gt;  &lt;img loading=&quot;lazy&quot; src=&quot;/sites/default/files/styles/original_dimensions/public/2025-08/Official%20EPO%20Map_1920x1080px_EU18_0.jpg?itok=X9IveWZO&quot; width=&quot;4000&quot; height=&quot;2250&quot; alt=&quot;Initial participating states (17)&quot; class=&quot;image-style-original-dimensions&quot;&gt;


&lt;/div&gt;
      
  &lt;/article&gt;
&lt;p&gt;&lt;br&gt;The 18 states in &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.epo.org/en/applying/european/unitary/unitary-patent/legal-framework&quot; target=&quot;_blank&quot;&gt;enhanced cooperation&lt;/a&gt; which already ratified the Agreements and participate in the Unitary Patent are: Austria, Belgium, Bulgaria, Denmark, Estonia, Finland, France, Germany, Italy, Latvia, Lithuania, Luxembourg, Malta, the Netherlands, Portugal, Romania, Slovenia, Sweden.&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/dd&gt;&lt;/dl&gt;&lt;h2&gt;Reducing complexity and lowering costs&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;p&gt;Since the EPC's entry into force on 7 October 1977, inventors and innovating companies have been able to obtain European patents by means of a centralised patent granting procedure. The EPO grants a European patent after centralised examination of the European patent application, saving inventors and innovating companies the cost of filing national patent applications with the national offices of EPC contracting states while ensuring the high quality of the patents granted.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;However, a European patent granted by the EPO must be validated and maintained individually in each country where the patent proprietor wishes it to take effect. This is a complex and potentially very costly process: validation requirements differ between countries and can lead to high direct and indirect costs, including translation costs, validation fees (i.e. fees due in some member states for publication of the translations) and associated representation costs, such as the attorney fees charged for the administration of the patent (i.e. payment of national renewal fees). These costs can be considerable and depend on the number of countries where the patent proprietor wishes to validate the European patent.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Unitary Patents remove the need for complex and costly national validation procedures.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;ul&gt;&lt;li data-list-item-id=&quot;e137eb2f6e3669752f7870f477f925096&quot;&gt;The EPO is in charge of the technical operation of the Unitary Patent and acts as a one-stop-shop, allowing for a simple registration of a Unitary Patent.&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li data-list-item-id=&quot;efeb76931021dc98d7ff9346e904e1ac8&quot;&gt;No fees are due for the filing and examination of the request for unitary effect or for registration of a Unitary Patent.&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li data-list-item-id=&quot;ed573097b8ca8552fbdc8ed6e58b68706&quot;&gt;No post-grant translations will be required after a six-year transitional period. During this period, a translation will be required for information only and will not have any legal effect.&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li data-list-item-id=&quot;ea79b913a5dcc12fd2a3f9284b10d0344&quot;&gt;For EU-based SMEs, natural persons, non-profit organisations, universities and public research organisations a new compensation scheme covers costs related to the translation of the patent application if it was filed in an official EU language other than English, French or German. They are paid a lump sum of EUR 500 when their Unitary Patent is registered.&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li data-list-item-id=&quot;e22aba7a79ec1e5706afb8acea9bc9989&quot;&gt;Unitary Patents are not subject to the currently fragmented renewal fee system: there is only one procedure and one deadline, all payments are made in euros and there is no obligation to use a representative.&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li data-list-item-id=&quot;e9a0e1d27a5b13df20b230646838a7498&quot;&gt;The renewal fees have been set at a very competitive level and are particularly attractive for the first ten years, the average lifetime of a European patent. Applicants also save on indirect costs. The more countries a patent proprietor wants to validate their European patent in, the greater the savings by opting for a Unitary Patent.&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li data-list-item-id=&quot;ed69b487e2f3c1935a20281582690e6a6&quot;&gt;All post-grant administration is handled centrally by the EPO, further reducing costs and administrative formalities.&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li data-list-item-id=&quot;e8ae2aa4490106b2439732b2bc42a5959&quot;&gt;The online register includes information on the legal status of Unitary Patents – notably on licences and transfers. This encourages technology transfer and investment in innovation.&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li data-list-item-id=&quot;ef521e8e81c059696b157ac5cc4a419e5&quot;&gt;Unitary Patents confer truly uniform protection since the substantive patent law – particularly the rights conferred by the Unitary Patent and any limitations of these rights and the remedies available in cases of infringement – has been harmonised in the UPC Agreement.&lt;/li&gt;&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;blue-background panel-card&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;panel-card-header&quot;&gt;&lt;h4 class=&quot;icon-eye&quot;&gt;See also&lt;/h4&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;panel-card-content&quot;&gt;&lt;a class=&quot;icon-link_internal&quot; href=&quot;https://www.epo.org/en/legal/up-upc/travaux-preparatoires-and-upc&quot;&gt;Travaux préparatoires to UP and UPC&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;" class="elastic">

      <path fill="#cddc39" style="fill: var(--color3, #cddc39)" d="M47.885 15.841l-11.92 21.077c-0.007 0.015-0.007 0.025 0 0.035l7.589 13.872c0.149 0.299 0.156 0.6 0.012 0.831-0.139 0.221-0.388 0.344-0.715 0.344h-5.377c-0.823 0-1.237-0.547-1.503-1.028 0 0-7.632-13.997-7.652-14.033 0.381-0.675 11.979-21.245 11.979-21.245 0.288-0.517 0.64-1.027 1.441-1.027h5.443c0.323 0 0.579 0.121 0.715 0.345 0.141 0.229 0.136 0.529-0.012 0.829z"></path>
    </symbol>
    <symbol id="truth" viewBox="0 0 144 144">
      <path d="M140.401 45V13.5H53.1006V129.9H90.6006V45H140.401Z" fill="#5448EE"></path>
      <path d="M3 13.5H39V45H3V13.5Z" fill="#5448EE"></path>
      <path d="M104.399 98.3994H140.399V129.899H104.399V98.3994Z" fill="#2FEECC"></path>
    </symbol>
    <symbol id="bluesky" viewBox="0 0 600 530" xml:space="preserve" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
      <rect width="600" height="530" fill="#1185fe"></rect>
      <path fill="#ffffff" d="m135.72 44.03c66.496 49.921 138.02 151.14 164.28 205.46 26.262-54.316 97.782-155.54 164.28-205.46 47.98-36.021 125.72-63.892 125.72 24.795 0 17.712-10.155 148.79-16.111 170.07-20.703 73.984-96.144 92.854-163.25 81.433 117.3 19.964 147.14 86.092 82.697 152.22-122.39 125.59-175.91-31.511-189.63-71.766-2.514-7.3797-3.6904-10.832-3.7077-7.8964-0.0174-2.9357-1.1937 0.51669-3.7077 7.8964-13.714 40.255-67.233 197.36-189.63 71.766-64.444-66.128-34.605-132.26 82.697-152.22-67.108 11.421-142.55-7.4491-163.25-81.433-5.9562-21.282-16.111-152.36-16.111-170.07 0-88.687 77.742-60.816 125.72-24.795z"></path>

            <!-- Title -->
            <h1 class="mb-5">
              Unitary Patent
            </h1>

            <!-- Body -->
            
            <div class="clearfix text-formatted field field--name-body field--type-text-with-summary field--label-hidden field__item"><p><em>Last updated: June 2026</em></p><div><div class="row"><div class="col-xs-12 col-sm-7"><article class="media media--type-remote-video media--view-mode-ratio-16-9-blazy">
  
      
            <div class="field field--name-field-media-oembed-video field--type-string field--label-hidden field__item youtube-cookies__iframe-container"><button class="youtube-cookies__thumbnail" data-yc-fake-button="1" aria-label="Open the YouTube cookie consent pop-up"><svg class="youtube_cookies__play-icon" height="100%" viewBox="0 0 68 48" width="100%"><path class="youtube_cookies__play-icon__fill" d="M66.52,7.74c-0.78-2.93-2.49-5.41-5.42-6.19C55.79,.13,34,0,34,0S12.21,.13,6.9,1.55 C3.97,2.33,2.27,4.81,1.48,7.74C0.06,13.05,0,24,0,24s0.06,10.95,1.48,16.26c0.78,2.93,2.49,5.41,5.42,6.19 C12.21,47.87,34,48,34,48s21.79-0.13,27.1-1.55c2.93-0.78,4.64-3.26,5.42-6.19C67.94,34.95,68,24,68,24S67.94,13.05,66.52,7.74z" fill="#f00"></path><path d="M 45,24 27,14 27,34" fill="#fff"></path></svg><picture class="youtube-cookies__thumbnail__picture"><source srcset="https://i.ytimg.com/vi_webp/LWYokuFRFX0/hqdefault.webp" type="image/webp"><img class="youtube-cookies__thumbnail__img" src="https://i.ytimg.com/vi/LWYokuFRFX0/hqdefault.jpg" width="480" height="360" alt="Video thumbnail"></picture></button><iframe src="" width="200" height="113" class="media-oembed-content youtube-cookies__iframe youtube-cookies__iframe--oembed" loading="lazy" title="The Unitary Patent" data-src="https://www.epo.org/en/media/oembed?url=https%3A//www.youtube.com/watch%3Fv%3DLWYokuFRFX0&amp;max_width=0&amp;max_height=0&amp;hash=X1Apwp_Q8AgDKpwDFc7-gSxlzaBtLGorq6zLh-c0cBo" data-yc-iframe-index="0" data-yc-iframe-processed="1"></iframe>
</div>
      
  </article>
</div><div class="col-xs-12 col-sm-5"><div><div class="promo-card"><article class="media media--type-image media--view-mode-ratio-3">
  
      
  <div class="field field--name-field-media-image field--type-image field--label-visually_hidden">
    <div class="field__label visually-hidden">Image</div>
              <div class="field__item">  <img loading="lazy" src="/sites/default/files/styles/ratio_3_1/public/2025-04/Unitary_Patent_Social_Media_Campaign_Ask.UP_Still_Cropped.jpg?h=b09aae90&amp;itok=148G9VxV" width="1920" height="640" alt="Decorative image" class="image-style-ratio-3-1">


</div>
          </div>

  </article>
<div class="promo-card-header"><h3>Ask.UP</h3></div><div class="promo-card-content"><p>Mini web-series where we clarify the most common questions about the Unitary Patent</p></div><div class="promo-card-footer"><a class="secondary-button button-icon icon-link_external" href="https://www.youtube.com/playlist?list=PLgDlswd0__eJRyOeAwG20xJ3i3DtyUzx9" role="button">Watch now</a></div></div></div></div></div></div><p>&nbsp;</p><p>European patents with unitary effect registered by the EPO on or after 1 September 2024 will take effect in the 18 EU Member States participating in enhanced co‑operation and having ratified the Agreement on a Unified Patent Court (UPC Agreement) by that date. It is expected that more EU Member States will ratify the UPC Agreement in the coming years, so that eventually Unitary Patents will make it possible to get patent protection in up to <a href="https://www.epo.org/en/applying/european/unitary/unitary-patent/legal-framework">25 EU Member States</a> by submitting a single request to the EPO.</p><p>The new Unitary Patent is based on the European patent granted by the EPO under the rules of the <a href="https://www.epo.org/law-practice/legal-texts/epc.html" target="_blank">European Patent Convention (EPC)</a>, so nothing changes in the pre-grant phase and the same high standards of quality search and examination apply. After a European patent is granted, the patent proprietor can request unitary effect, thereby getting a European patent with unitary effect (Unitary Patent) that provides uniform patent protection in 18 EU Member States.</p><div class="inline-buttons"><a class="primary-button button-icon mdi mdi-arrow-right" href="https://www.epo.org/en/service-support/faq/law-practice/unitary-patent" role="button" tabindex="-1">Frequently asked questions about the Unitary Patent</a></div><h2>Territorial scope of European patents and those with unitary effect</h2><div class="ckeditor-accordion-container" data-once="EpoAccordion"><div class="accordion__expand text-right"><button class="button secondary-button js-accordion-expand" data-once="accordionExpand">Expand all</button></div><dl class="styled" data-once="ckeditorAccordions"><dt><a class="ckeditor-accordion-toggler" href="#MemberstatesoftheEPO40" id="MemberstatesoftheEPO40" onclick="return false;"><span class="ckeditor-accordion-toggle" data-once="EpoDropDownEPC"></span>Member states of the EPO (40)</a></dt><dd><article class="media media--type-image media--view-mode-ratio-16-9-blazy">
  
      
            <div class="blazy blazy--photoswipe blazy--lightbox blazy--photoswipe-gallery blazy--field blazy--field-media-image blazy--field-media-image--ratio-16-9-blazy field field--name-field-media-image field--type-image field--label-hidden field__item is-blazy photoswipe--on" data-photoswipe-gallery="" data-blazy="" data-once="blazy blazy-photoswipe">




          <a href="/sites/default/files/2026-06/Map%20WEB%20No%20CC%20-%20Official%20EPO%20Member%20States.jpg" class="blazy__photoswipe litebox litebox--image" data-photoswipe-trigger="" data-b-media="{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;photoswipe&quot;,&quot;count&quot;:1,&quot;boxType&quot;:&quot;image&quot;,&quot;type&quot;:&quot;image&quot;,&quot;token&quot;:&quot;b-511b72601d7&quot;,&quot;paddingHack&quot;:true,&quot;width&quot;:1920,&quot;height&quot;:1080}" data-once="videoBlazy videoBlazyClick">    <div data-b-token="b-511b72601d7" class="media media--blazy media--switch media--switch--photoswipe media--image  is-b-loaded"><img alt="Map showing the potential geographic coverage of European patents" decoding="async" class="media__element b-lazy b-loaded" loading="lazy" src="/sites/default/files/styles/ratio_16_9/public/2026-06/Map%20WEB%20No%20CC%20-%20Official%20EPO%20Member%20States.jpg?h=920929c4&amp;itok=utbdQaJ3" width="1920" height="1080">
        </div><div class="magnify-plus"><a class="icon-magnify-plus-outline"></a></div></a>
        
        
  </div>
      
  </article>
<p><br>The EPO currently has <a href="https://www.epo.org/en/about-us/foundation/member-states" target="_blank">40 member states</a>, in addition to <a href="https://www.epo.org/en/about-us/foundation/extension-states" target="_blank">one extension state</a> and <a href="https://www.epo.org/en/about-us/foundation/validation-states" target="_blank">five validation states</a>.</p></dd><dt><a class="ckeditor-accordion-toggler" href="#Statesinenhancedcooperation25" id="Statesinenhancedcooperation25" onclick="return false;"><span class="ckeditor-accordion-toggle" data-once="EpoDropDownEPC"></span>States in enhanced co-operation (25)</a></dt><dd><article class="media media--type-image media--view-mode-original-dimensions-no-blazy">
  
      
            <div class="field field--name-field-media-image field--type-image field--label-hidden field__item">  <img loading="lazy" src="/sites/default/files/styles/original_dimensions/public/2025-08/Official%20EPO%20Map_1920x1080px_EU25_0.jpg?itok=ciW2Czws" width="4000" height="2250" alt="States in enhanced cooperation (25)" class="image-style-original-dimensions">


</div>
      
  </article>
<p><br>The 25 states participating in <a href="https://www.epo.org/en/applying/european/unitary/unitary-patent/legal-framework">enhanced cooperation</a> to bring about the Unitary Patent system are: Austria, Belgium, Bulgaria, Cyprus, Czech Republic, Denmark, Estonia, Finland, France, Germany, Greece, Hungary, Ireland, Italy, Latvia, Lithuania, Luxembourg, Malta, the Netherlands, Poland, Portugal, Romania, Slovakia, Slovenia, Sweden.</p></dd><dt><a class="ckeditor-accordion-toggler" href="#CurrentstatesintheUPandUPCsystem18" id="CurrentstatesintheUPandUPCsystem18" onclick="return false;"><span class="ckeditor-accordion-toggle" data-once="EpoDropDownEPC"></span>Current states in the UP and UPC system (18)</a></dt><dd><article class="media media--type-image media--view-mode-original-dimensions-no-blazy">
  
      
            <div class="field field--name-field-media-image field--type-image field--label-hidden field__item">  <img loading="lazy" src="/sites/default/files/styles/original_dimensions/public/2025-08/Official%20EPO%20Map_1920x1080px_EU18_0.jpg?itok=X9IveWZO" width="4000" height="2250" alt="Initial participating states (17)" class="image-style-original-dimensions">


</div>
      
  </article>
<p><br>The 18 states in <a href="https://www.epo.org/en/applying/european/unitary/unitary-patent/legal-framework" target="_blank">enhanced cooperation</a> which already ratified the Agreements and participate in the Unitary Patent are: Austria, Belgium, Bulgaria, Denmark, Estonia, Finland, France, Germany, Italy, Latvia, Lithuania, Luxembourg, Malta, the Netherlands, Portugal, Romania, Slovenia, Sweden.&nbsp;</p></dd></dl></div><h2>Reducing complexity and lowering costs</h2><p>Since the EPC's entry into force on 7 October 1977, inventors and innovating companies have been able to obtain European patents by means of a centralised patent granting procedure. The EPO grants a European patent after centralised examination of the European patent application, saving inventors and innovating companies the cost of filing national patent applications with the national offices of EPC contracting states while ensuring the high quality of the patents granted.</p><p>However, a European patent granted by the EPO must be validated and maintained individually in each country where the patent proprietor wishes it to take effect. This is a complex and potentially very costly process: validation requirements differ between countries and can lead to high direct and indirect costs, including translation costs, validation fees (i.e. fees due in some member states for publication of the translations) and associated representation costs, such as the attorney fees charged for the administration of the patent (i.e. payment of national renewal fees). These costs can be considerable and depend on the number of countries where the patent proprietor wishes to validate the European patent.</p><p>Unitary Patents remove the need for complex and costly national validation procedures.</p><ul><li data-list-item-id="e137eb2f6e3669752f7870f477f925096">The EPO is in charge of the technical operation of the Unitary Patent and acts as a one-stop-shop, allowing for a simple registration of a Unitary Patent.</li><li data-list-item-id="efeb76931021dc98d7ff9346e904e1ac8">No fees are due for the filing and examination of the request for unitary effect or for registration of a Unitary Patent.</li><li data-list-item-id="ed573097b8ca8552fbdc8ed6e58b68706">No post-grant translations will be required after a six-year transitional period. During this period, a translation will be required for information only and will not have any legal effect.</li><li data-list-item-id="ea79b913a5dcc12fd2a3f9284b10d0344">For EU-based SMEs, natural persons, non-profit organisations, universities and public research organisations a new compensation scheme covers costs related to the translation of the patent application if it was filed in an official EU language other than English, French or German. They are paid a lump sum of EUR 500 when their Unitary Patent is registered.</li><li data-list-item-id="e22aba7a79ec1e5706afb8acea9bc9989">Unitary Patents are not subject to the currently fragmented renewal fee system: there is only one procedure and one deadline, all payments are made in euros and there is no obligation to use a representative.</li><li data-list-item-id="e9a0e1d27a5b13df20b230646838a7498">The renewal fees have been set at a very competitive level and are particularly attractive for the first ten years, the average lifetime of a European patent. Applicants also save on indirect costs. The more countries a patent proprietor wants to validate their European patent in, the greater the savings by opting for a Unitary Patent.</li><li data-list-item-id="ed69b487e2f3c1935a20281582690e6a6">All post-grant administration is handled centrally by the EPO, further reducing costs and administrative formalities.</li><li data-list-item-id="e8ae2aa4490106b2439732b2bc42a5959">The online register includes information on the legal status of Unitary Patents – notably on licences and transfers. This encourages technology transfer and investment in innovation.</li><li data-list-item-id="ef521e8e81c059696b157ac5cc4a419e5">Unitary Patents confer truly uniform protection since the substantive patent law – particularly the rights conferred by the Unitary Patent and any limitations of these rights and the remedies available in cases of infringement – has been harmonised in the UPC Agreement.</li></ul><p>&nbsp;</p><div class="blue-background panel-card"><div class="panel-card-header"><h4 class="icon-eye">See also</h4></div><div class="panel-card-content"><a class="icon-link_internal" href="https://www.epo.org/en/legal/up-upc/travaux-preparatoires-and-upc">Travaux préparatoires to UP and UPC</a></div></div></div>
      
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</article>

      </div>
